The agent that wrote the prompts stops seeing their flaws, so a FRESH reviewer (a subagent, a second session, another model or a person) gets only the references, the frames and one intent line per frame, never the prompts or our reasoning. It scores the batch 1-10 on eight fixed categories, names at most five fixes ranked by damage, and gives every frame a keep / fix / reject verdict. A batch passes at no category below 6 and a mean of 7 or more; rounds are logged, capped, and stopped when three rounds in a row fail… ([teen-boop/AiProductionPipeline](https://github.com/teen-boop/AiProductionPipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/independent-frame-critic) · ★6)
- **independent-fresh-context-review**: Get a genuinely independent second opinion on finished work (code, a report, a plan, a design, research) by handing a written brief to a fresh-context reviewer agent that never saw how the work was produced. Use when the user wants an independent, fresh-eyes, or second-opinion review before shipping. Not for a normal diff review in the current context (code-review) or for acting on review feedback (receiving-code-review). ([XposeMarket/PromSRC](https://github.com/XposeMarket/PromSRC/tree/HEAD/generated/bundled-skills/independent-fresh-context-review) · 2 copies)
- **independent-governance-review**: Follow the current Review Guideline, Prompt, and Request. Confirm the reviewer is distinct from proposal preparer, applier, and verifier, and that the independent Verification result is PASS. Resolve all reports and the current repository state. ([ScalableParimal/SupportIQ](https://github.com/ScalableParimal/SupportIQ/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/independent-governance-review) · 5 copies)
- **independent-governance-verification**: Follow the current Verification Guideline, Prompt, and Request. Establish actor identity and prove it differs from the preparer, applier, and reviewer. Resolve the exact proposal, Human Approval, application report, repository state, and target evidence. ([ScalableParimal/Governed-SDLC-Process](https://github.com/ScalableParimal/Governed-SDLC-Process/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/independent-governance-verification) · 5 copies)
- **independent-hypothesis-critic**: Independent read-only critic for a Hypothesis Forge CRITIC_INPUT_PACKET. Maximizes early honest kill of weak hypotheses. Use when Forge auto-handoff launches critic in new context, or when the owner explicitly invokes /independent-hypothesis-critic with a packet. Never co-author Forge; never run experiments or mutate Git. ([lancerbeta/solana-alpha-lab](https://github.com/lancerbeta/solana-alpha-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/independent-hypothesis-critic))
- **independent-hypothesis-weighting-multiple-testing**: Use when you have a large family of test p-values (e.g., ~20k genes from edgeR or 50 camera gene-set tests) plus an independent continuous covariate like average expression, and you need FDR-controlled calls (FDR &lt; 0.1 or &lt; 0.05) with maximal power. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/independent-hypothesis-weighting-multiple-testing))
